# EXTRACTION-MANIFEST - TASK-078 (payload extraction from the .dsh session transcripts)

Generated by `scripts/extract_pass1.py` -> `scripts/extract_pass2_v3.py` -> `scripts/fix_candidates.py` -> `scripts/build_manifest.py`.
Payload source: `C:\Users\wyl\AppData\Local\Temp\mcp-recovery\transcripts\*.jsonl` (178 files, 594,263,811 B).
Staging root: `C:\Users\wyl\AppData\Local\Temp\mcp-recovery\staging\`. Nothing was written to `F:`.

## 0. Column meanings

* `path` - staging-relative path. In-repo paths are relative to `F:\RustProjects\godot-mcp-pro\code\godot\`
  (the deleted tree); anything else is parked under `__external\<drive><abs path>`.
* `cand` - which candidate produced the staged bytes:
  `write-chain` = write payload + replayed edits; `read-epoch` = version-coherent read-window merge;
  `read-complete` = a single complete read that supersedes the chain; `-` = nothing usable.
* `conf` - **高** = direct `write` (or write+edit replay that a later complete read confirms);
  **中** = rebuilt from `read` windows and/or an edit chain that cannot be replayed with zero failures;
  **低** = only fragments (diff/partial reads) or a replay with many failed edits / parse errors.
* `bytes` - size of the staged file. `tail` - `no` = complete, `yes` = end of file missing,
  `partial-only` = interior lines missing but the tail is present.
* `cov%` - union coverage of the read windows against the newest observed `totalLines`.
* `src` - transcript file (basename prefix) + record `seq`/`time` of the last authoritative payload.

## 10. Companion artifacts (declared, none of them on F:)

* `staging\__history\<path>\<NNNN>_<src>_seq<S>_<sha8>.bak` - every superseded version (4543 files).
* `staging\__candidates\<path>.{write-chain,read-epoch}.txt` - the rejected candidate wherever the two differed (296 files).
* `staging\__diffs\` - diff-class payloads (git diff/show/status), one `.diff` + one `.cmd.txt` per captured command, plus `INDEX.md`.
* `staging\__payload-index\` - the raw machine-readable spool (`events-{write,edit,read,diff,termfile,termdump,termlog,misc}.jsonl`).
* `work\` - intermediate analysis output (`reconstruction.jsonl`, `gen-runs.jsonl`, `ps1-parse.json`, `swaps.json`).
* `scripts\` - the extraction scripts (see the report for the inventory).
